**Why it feels beautiful**
- Excellent hygiene on classic debt classes: dead exports/unused/logs/duplication/security are basically cleaned.
- Good local craftsmanship in core primitives like `/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/lib/queryKeys/tasks.ts` and `/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/lib/errorHandling/handleError.ts`.
- Strong test surface area (`871` test files for `1853` TS/TSX files).

**Why it feels ugly**
- Core orchestration files are too large and too central:
- `/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/ShotEditor/index.tsx` (`1122` LOC)
- `/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/components/InlineSegmentVideo.tsx` (`786` LOC)
- `/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/app/App.tsx:75` (monster function + deep provider stack)
- Interface bloat: many 30–47 field prop contracts in MediaGallery/MediaLightbox areas.
- Information architecture is flat in critical places (e.g. `/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/components` has `113` files).
- Pattern drift: competing settings approaches (`useAutoSaveSettings`, `usePersistentToolState`, `useToolSettings`).
- Chronic reopening debt: `601` findings keep reappearing.
- Operational quality drift:
- `lint` fails (7 errors, e.g. `/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/components/MediaGalleryItem.test.tsx:295`, `/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/supabase/functions/complete_task/generation-child.ts:345`)
- `test` has `29` failures across `12` files (e.g. `/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/providers/__tests__/RealtimeProvider.test.tsx:92`, `/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/app/main.test.tsx:1`)
- `build` passes, but with large-chunk and dynamic-import warnings (main bundle `~2.68MB` minified).

**Boundary judgment**
- Inside modules: often thoughtful and intentional.
- Between modules: too much wiring, too many broad contracts, too many “god surfaces.”
- At system scale: maintainable with effort, but not yet graceful.

PLEASE IMPLEMENT THIS PLAN:
# Reigh Structural Recovery Program (Architecture-First, 8–12 Weeks)

## Summary
This plan is a **single-integration-branch architecture program** to eliminate structural debt that is currently driving chronic reopeners, brittle tests, and oversized orchestration modules.  
It is designed to be **thorough but non-vanity**: every refactor must produce measurable structural, reliability, or performance improvement.

Current baseline from your repo (2026-02-17):
- `desloppify`: 818 open findings, strict `96.4`
- Heavy classes: `smells` 244, `props` 119, `structural` 87, `subjective_review` 274
- Chronic reopeners: 601
- Runtime checks: `lint` failing, `test` 29 failures, `build` passes with chunk and import warnings

Chosen strategy (from your inputs):
- Priority: **Architecture first**
- Interface posture: **Aggressive reshape allowed**
- Horizon: **8–12 weeks**
- Scope: **Frontend-first with backend gates**
- Delivery style: **All in one go** (single merge at end)

---

## Program Guardrails (Anti-Vanity Refactor Rules)
A code change is in-scope only if it satisfies at least one of these outcomes:
- Removes a current failing check (`lint`, `test`, `build`, `edge test`)
- Reduces detector debt in target files (`smells`, `props`, `structural`, `patterns`, `flat_dirs`, `test_coverage`)
- Eliminates a chronic reopener in `desloppify show --chronic`
- Reduces runtime or bundle risk (chunk size, duplicate import paths, unstable async flow)
- Reduces public surface complexity (props/state contract shrink, provider-depth reduction, query-key normalization)

A code change is out-of-scope if:
- It only moves code without reducing complexity, coupling, or defect risk
- It rewrites stable modules with no detector signal and no operational pain
- It introduces new abstractions with single call-sites and no near-term reuse

---

## Success Criteria (Decision-Complete Targets)

## Quality Gates (must all pass before final merge)
- `npm run lint` passes with zero errors
- `npm run test -- --reporter=dot` passes
- `npm run test:edge` passes
- `npm run build` passes with no new warnings beyond approved exceptions
- `desloppify scan --path src/` run at each phase gate and final gate

## Structural Targets (end-state)
- `smells` open: from 244 to <= 80
- `props` open: from 119 to <= 30
- `structural` open: from 87 to <= 25
- `patterns` open: from 2 to 0
- `flat_dirs` open: from 15 to <= 5
- `test_coverage` open: from 53 to <= 15
- `chronic` reopeners: from 601 to <= 120
- Strict health: >= 97.5 with no score-gaming warnings unresolved

## Module Shape Targets
- No orchestration component > 500 LOC unless explicitly exempted
- No prop contract > 20 fields in active UI flow components
- No provider tree deeper than 8 in app entry path
- No test importing side-effect bootstrap entrypoints directly

---

## Public API / Interface Changes (Explicit)
These are intentional and approved under the aggressive-refactor posture.

1. Introduce app bootstrap boundary
- Add `renderApp(root: HTMLElement)` in `/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/app/bootstrap.tsx`
- Keep `/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/app/main.tsx` as thin runtime entrypoint
- Update tests to import bootstrap-safe entrypoints, not `main.tsx`

2. Introduce provider composition boundary
- Add `/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/app/providers/AppProviders.tsx`
- Collapse direct provider nesting in `/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/app/App.tsx`
- Preserve external app behavior, but reshape internal provider wiring API

3. Normalize query-key public contract
- Canonical source stays `/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/lib/queryKeys/tasks.ts`
- Remove legacy test imports from `@/shared/lib/queryKeys`
- Add compatibility aliases only where required during migration, then remove by final phase

4. Standardize error handling public contract
- Canonical path: `@/shared/lib/errorHandling/handleError`
- Remove usage of `@/shared/lib/errorHandler` in tests and runtime modules
- Align error option contracts in hooks relying on `handleError`

5. Replace broad prop contracts with scoped view models
- Media and ShotEditor domains move from huge prop interfaces to grouped domain contracts
- Examples impacted:
- `/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/components/MediaLightbox/ImageLightbox.tsx`
- `/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/components/MediaLightbox/VideoLightbox.tsx`
- `/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/ShotEditor/index.tsx`

6. Unify tool settings contract
- Standardize on one settings access/write path
- Replace competing patterns from:
- `useAutoSaveSettings`
- `usePersistentToolState`
- `useToolSettings`
- Update docs in `/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/docs/structure_detail/settings_system.md`

Use one long-lived integration branch:
- Branch: `codex/architecture-recovery`

- Internal governance:
- Weekly checkpoint tags (`checkpoint/wk1` ... `checkpoint/wk10`)
- Weekly freeze windows for rebase/conflict cleanup
- Weekly full quality gate runs
- Weekly desloppify trend snapshots committed in `/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/docs/quality/`

## Detailed Workstream Mapping (Issue Class -> Concrete Owner Stream)
1. `smells::monster_function`
- Owner stream: App shell + tool decomposition + media decomposition
- Primary files include `/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/app/App.tsx` and travel/media hotspots

2. `props::bloated`
- Owner stream: contract collapse
- Primary files include media gallery/lightbox and shot editor section contracts

3. `structural::needs_decomposition`
- Owner stream: architecture segmentation
- Primary files include ShotEditor, HeroSection, GlobalHeader, InlineSegmentVideo

4. `patterns::tool_settings`
- Owner stream: settings unification
- Primary files under `/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/hooks/`

5. `test_coverage::*transitive_only`
- Owner stream: direct test additions and deterministic harness

6. `chronic` reopeners
- Owner stream: weekly chronic elimination list (top 25 each week)

---

## Weekly Cadence (Non-Negotiable)
- Monday: select top chronic 25 + hotspot set for the week
- Tuesday–Thursday: implementation blocks
- Friday morning: full quality gate run
- Friday afternoon: checkpoint tag + metrics snapshot + next-week plan update

---

## Test Cases and Scenarios (Required)
1. App boot and provider assembly
- Bootstrap rendering through `renderApp` with and without session
- Realtime provider state transitions and reconnect path
- No DOM-root side effect failures in tests

2. Shot editor lifecycle
- Load shot, update settings, generate, join segments, navigate shots
- Verify action services called once, no duplicate mutation side effects
- Validate no stale settings writes under rapid input

3. Media lightbox/gallery behavior
- Open/close, mode switches, edit actions, task submission, variant promotion
- Verify reduced prop contracts preserve behavior parity

4. Settings consistency
- Same logical setting read/write path across tools
- Persistence precedence (shot > project > user > default) retained

5. Query-key and error contract compatibility
- Task cancellation invalidation keys
- Generation-task mapping cache keys
- Error handling path and option behaviors across hooks

6. Performance and bundling
- Critical route load timings before/after
- Bundle chunk integrity and warning checks
- No runtime regressions from lazy boundaries

7. Backend gate scenarios
- Edge test suite execution
- Client-edge contract flows not broken by frontend refactors

---

## Risk Management and Rollback Policy
- Use checkpoint tags for hard rollback points inside integration branch
- If a phase misses exit criteria by >1 week, freeze new refactors and run stabilization sprint
- If test failure count rises for 2 consecutive checkpoints, block architecture changes until recovered
- If chunk size increases after a decomposition phase, require compensating lazy-boundary changes before continuing

---

- All phase exits passed
- All quality gates green
- Docs updated to match final architecture
- `desloppify show --chronic` reduced to target
- No unresolved score integrity warnings
- Change log summary generated in `/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/docs/quality/final-architecture-recovery-report.md`

## Assumptions and Defaults Chosen
- Architecture-first sequencing is intentional even if short-term test churn increases
- Aggressive interface reshaping is permitted and expected
- Plan horizon fixed at 8–12 weeks
- Refactor focus is frontend `src/`, with backend/edge validation gates at each checkpoint
- Delivery is one final merge from a long-lived `codex/` integration branch
- Generated files such as `/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/integrations/supabase/types.ts` are not manually refactored unless regeneration workflow requires it

Implemented another full architecture/refactor pass and kept pushing without pausing.

**What I changed**
- Decomposed `InlineSegmentVideo` into focused modules and replaced smoke tests with behavior tests.
- Added direct tests for all new inline-segment modules to avoid transitive-only coverage regressions.
- Fixed a real bug in layout style generation (invalid `calc(undefined%...)`) by guarding absolute style creation.
- Decomposed `HeroSection` and extracted animated CTA content.
- Replaced hardcoded hero color literals with CSS token usage.
- Reduced `HoverScrubVideo` below structural threshold and removed dead/no-op handlers.
- Collapsed MediaGallery prop-contract bloat by splitting large flat interfaces into scoped contracts in:
  - grid
  - header
  - lightbox
- Standardized several chronic `console.error` paths to canonical `handleError` contracts:
  - realtime connection/auth failure paths
  - not-found route error path
  - app error boundary catch path
- Removed dead internal responsive helper code (`void`-suppressed symbols) in `useContentResponsive`.
